# Daily video prompt pack — 19/11/2026 · Rooster day (ថ្ងៃឆ្នាំរកា)

Lucky signs: Rooster, Ox, Snake · Careful: Rabbit
Day element: Yin Fire 🔥 (Ding-you day; ធាតុភ្លើង) · every action scene today carries Fire magic: warm red-gold embers, small dancing flame-petals and rising sparks that never burn anything.
Format: vertical 9:16 (YouTube Shorts, Facebook Reels, TikTok), about 45 seconds: 4 Veo scenes of about 8 seconds, then a lucky-numbers scene of about 10–12 seconds.
Two versions: make the pictures once, then record a Khmer voice-over and an English voice-over (two videos, or one video with both subtitle tracks).
Meaning scenes: in the love, work, money and horoscope scenes the zodiac guardian and its animal act out what Chanda is saying, the guardian protecting and caring and the animal trusting (no real-life people), and every scene with Chanda on camera says which feeling she shows.
Ratings, lucky signs and lucky numbers come from the site's fixed rules and match the website's daily fortune for this date.
Voice-over wording: standard script (this pack was made ahead; the site's own daily wording appears the day before).

## Today's horoscope – all 12 signs
Each sign's prediction for 19/11/2026: general luck, love, work, money and advice, in English and Khmer, ready to copy into your Facebook, TikTok, Telegram and YouTube posts. The videos speak the same predictions: the 60-second video (the Rooster's luck and love), the 180-second video (love, work and money), the long videos (every sign) and the full-fortune video. Lucky numbers are given only for today's lucky signs, as in the videos.
Wording: the website's standard lines for each sign's rating (this date's written fortune is published on the website the day before; open the Studio at mybirthsign.com/studio/ then to use it).

### Khmer
🐀 ឆ្នាំជូត – ថ្ងៃធម្មតា
ទូទៅ៖ ថ្ងៃនេះ សូមធ្វើអ្វីៗឲ្យសាមញ្ញ។ ជំហានតូចៗ និងនឹងនរ ល្អជាងការលោតធំៗ។
ស្នេហា៖ ជាថ្ងៃស្ងប់ស្ងាត់សម្រាប់ទំនាក់ទំនង។ ការយកចិត្តទុកដាក់តូចៗ មានន័យជាងផែនការធំៗ។
ការងារ៖ ជាថ្ងៃល្អសម្រាប់រៀបចំផែនការ និងកិច្ចការតូចៗ ជាជាងការផ្លាស់ប្ដូរធំៗ។
លុយកាក់៖ ការចំណាយប្រចាំថ្ងៃ គួររក្សាឲ្យសាមញ្ញ។ សូមប្រៀបធៀបតម្លៃមុនពេលទិញ។
ដំបូន្មាន៖ សូមធ្វើការដោយស្ងប់ៗ ហើយបញ្ចប់អ្វីដែលបានចាប់ផ្ដើម មុននឹងចាប់ផ្ដើមរឿងថ្មី។

## Luckiest animal today – Rooster (រកា) · Great Day
One hero post for the day's luckiest animal: a picture, a short video and a caption. Lucky color brown (ត្នោត) · best time 01:00–03:00 · lucky numbers 7 · 88 · 582 · 7788 · 88589 (for fun only).

### Picture prompt (square 1:1)
Create a photorealistic hero image of @Rooster (the Rooster guardian with their rooster), the Rooster of the Chinese zodiac, as a majestic celestial guardian from a high-budget fantasy epic. The rooster symbolizes honesty, confidence and a bright new day: a proud, confident and bright expression and posture that show this, mouth calm and closed. Realistic anatomy and textures, engraved gold ornaments with physically real metallic reflections. It stands in a garden of white jasmine and frangipani flowers in front of a temple inspired by Angkor Wat, beside a still reflecting pool, under an enormous luminous full moon and a deep indigo starry sky; moonbeams fall on the rooster, golden particles and flowing ribbons of liquid-gold light swirl around it. Cool silver-blue moonlight, warm golden rim light, volumetric mist, cinematic depth of field, ultra-detailed. Square 1:1, full body visible, three-quarter view, centered, with space around it. No text, no letters, no watermark, no logo, no cartoon or plastic look, no cropped body parts.

Herald of the dawn, the Rooster symbolizes honesty, confidence, and a bright new day. Let it guide you.

## 180-second video – YouTube
A 180-second video about the Rooster, today's luckiest animal: 18 clips of 10 seconds. Set the clip length in Flow to 10 seconds and add Chanda and the saved character @Rooster as ingredients. Chanda opens the video on camera with the hook and narrates the rest off screen. The animals never speak or move their mouths: they only act. For each scene, press "Copy + English voice" or "Copy + Khmer voice": it copies the style line, the scene and the voice together. Put the clips in order 1 to 18.

### Topic and audience
A three-minute guide to the Rooster's Great Day: meaning, character, love, work, money and lucky details. For people born in the Year of the Rooster and zodiac fans on YouTube Shorts.

### Opening hook – 5 options
★ 1. Daily fortune
   English: Born in the Year of the Rooster? Here's your lucky color, best time, and numbers for today, plus one gentle reminder.
   Khmer: អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំរកាមែនទេ? នេះគឺពណ៌ ពេលវេលា និងលេខសំណាងរបស់អ្នកថ្ងៃនេះ ព្រមទាំងការរំលឹកមួយផង។
2. Curiosity
   English: Out of all twelve zodiac signs, only one has a Great Day today. Is it yours?
   Khmer: ក្នុងចំណោមរាសីទាំង 12 មានតែមួយគត់ ដែលមានថ្ងៃល្អខ្លាំងនៅថ្ងៃនេះ។ តើជារាសីរបស់អ្នកឬទេ?
3. Personality
   English: If you were born in the Year of the Rooster, people may describe you as observant, hard-working, and confident. Today, that side of you shines.

## Voice-over – 180-second video
The same lines as the voice in each scene above, all together, for recording the voice yourself or with text-to-speech (then copy the scenes with plain "Copy", so Flow does not add a voice too).

### English
Scene 1 (0:00–0:10)
Born in the Year of the Rooster? Here's your lucky color, best time, and numbers for today, plus one gentle reminder.

Scene 2 (0:10–0:20)
Today, Thursday, 19 November 2026, is a Rooster day. It is a Great Day for everyone born in the Year of the Rooster. This is your day to shine!

Scene 3 (0:20–0:30)
Herald of the dawn, the Rooster symbolizes honesty, confidence, and a bright new day. Today, this good energy is with you.

Scene 4 (0:30–0:40)
People born in the Year of the Rooster are observant, hard-working, and confident. Roosters are detail-oriented and take pride in doing things well.

Scene 5 (0:40–0:50)
Born in the Year of the Rooster? Your luck is strong today. Stay proud, confident and bright. Say yes to good chances, and share your happiness with the people around you.

Scene 6 (0:50–1:00)
Love and family today: A good day to reach out, share a meal and show your affection openly.

Scene 7 (1:00–1:10)
Work today: Customers and coworkers respond well to you. A good day to present your ideas and close a deal.

Scene 8 (1:10–1:20)
Money today: A favourable day for steady income. Save a small part of today's earnings before treating yourself.

Scene 9 (1:20–1:30)
Your lucky color today is brown. Wear something brown, or carry a small brown item with you, to keep good luck close.

Scene 10 (1:30–1:40)
Your best time today is from 1 to 3 in the early morning. It is a good time for important plans, meetings, or a fresh start.

Scene 11 (1:40–1:50)
Your lucky direction today is the northeast. When you start something new, face the northeast, and walk forward with a calm and happy heart.

Scene 12 (1:50–2:00)
For lucky numbers: 7, 88, and 582. Comment your lucky number below, and let's say it again: 7, 88, 582!

Scene 13 (2:00–2:10)
The Rooster's lucky days of the week are Thursday and Saturday. Keep them in mind for plans that matter to you.

Scene 14 (2:10–2:20)
A gentle reminder: people born in the Year of the Rooster can be overly critical or blunt with others. Today, stay balanced, and listen to your heart.

Scene 15 (2:20–2:30)
Make a small wish today, and take one kind step toward it. A peaceful heart helps good luck find its way to you.

Scene 16 (2:30–2:40)
Before the day ends, take a quiet moment to say thank you. Thank the people who help you, and thank yourself for trying your best.

Scene 17 (2:40–2:50)
Let's remember today's luck. Lucky color: brown. Best time: from 1 to 3 in the early morning. Lucky direction: northeast. Lucky numbers: 7, 88, and 582.

Scene 18 (2:50–3:00)
That's your Chinese zodiac fortune for today. See all twelve signs and your lucky numbers at mybirthsign dot com. Follow us for tomorrow's fortune. Have a lucky day!

សូមរំលឹកបន្តិច៖ អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំរកា ជួនកាលថ្លែងយោបល់រិះគន់ខ្លាំង ឬត្រង់ពេក។ ថ្ងៃនេះ សូមរក្សាតុល្យភាព និងស្ដាប់ចិត្តខ្លួនឯង។

Scene 15 (2:20–2:30)
ថ្ងៃនេះ សូមប្រាថ្នាអ្វីមួយតូចៗ ហើយធ្វើរឿងល្អមួយ ដើម្បីឈានទៅរកក្ដីប្រាថ្នានោះ។ ចិត្តដែលស្ងប់ នឹងជួយនាំសំណាងល្អ មករកអ្នក។

Scene 16 (2:30–2:40)
មុនថ្ងៃនេះបញ្ចប់ សូមចំណាយពេលស្ងាត់មួយភ្លែត ដើម្បីអរគុណ។ អរគុណមនុស្សដែលជួយអ្នក ហើយអរគុណខ្លួនឯង ដែលបានព្យាយាមអស់ពីសមត្ថភាព។

Scene 17 (2:40–2:50)
សូមចងចាំសំណាងថ្ងៃនេះ។ ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌ត្នោត។ ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោងមួយ ដល់ម៉ោងបី ទៀបភ្លឺ។ ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសឦសាន។ លេខសំណាង៖ លេខប្រាំពីរ លេខប៉ែតសិបប្រាំបី និងលេខប្រាំរយប៉ែតសិបពីរ។

Scene 18 (2:50–3:00)
នេះគឺជាជោគជតារាសីចិន សម្រាប់ថ្ងៃនេះ។ អ្នកអាចមើលរាសីទាំងដប់ពីរ និងលេខសំណាង នៅលើគេហទំព័រ mybirthsign.com។ សូមតាមដានយើង សម្រាប់ជោគជតាថ្ងៃស្អែក។ សូមឲ្យអ្នកមានសំណាងល្អ!

1
00:00:00,000 --> 00:00:02,392

2
00:00:02,442 --> 00:00:07,392

3

ព្រមទាំងការរំលឹកមួយផង។

4

ថ្ងៃនេះ ថ្ងៃព្រហស្បតិ៍ ទី 19 ខែវិច្ឆិកា

5
00:00:12,910 --> 00:00:14,950
ឆ្នាំ 2026 គឺជាថ្ងៃឆ្នាំរកា។

6

7
00:00:18,134 --> 00:00:19,950
ថ្ងៃនេះ គឺជាថ្ងៃរបស់អ្នក!

8
00:00:20,000 --> 00:00:22,414
មាន់ គឺជាអ្នកប្រកាសពេលព្រឹកព្រលឹម។

9
00:00:22,464 --> 00:00:24,515
ឆ្នាំរកាតំណាងឲ្យភាពស្មោះត្រង់

10
00:00:24,565 --> 00:00:26,979
ទំនុកចិត្ត និងថ្ងៃថ្មីដ៏ភ្លឺស្វាង។

11
00:00:27,029 --> 00:00:29,950
ថ្ងៃនេះ ថាមពលល្អទាំងនេះ កំពុងនៅជាមួយអ្នក។

12
00:00:30,000 --> 00:00:32,295
អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំរកា ជាមនុស្សល្អិតល្អន់

13
00:00:32,345 --> 00:00:34,640
ឧស្សាហ៍ព្យាយាម និងជឿជាក់លើខ្លួនឯង។

14
00:00:34,690 --> 00:00:36,847
រកាយកចិត្តទុកដាក់លើព័ត៌មានលម្អិត

15
00:00:36,897 --> 00:00:39,950
និងមានមោទនភាពក្នុងការធ្វើការអ្វីមួយឱ្យបានល្អ។

16
00:00:40,000 --> 00:00:42,172
សម្រាប់អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំរកា ថ្ងៃនេះ

17
00:00:42,222 --> 00:00:44,172
សំណាងរបស់អ្នកល្អខ្លាំងណាស់។

18
00:00:44,222 --> 00:00:46,839
សូមរក្សាចិត្តឲ្យស្ងប់ ទទួលយកឱកាសល្អៗ

19
00:00:46,889 --> 00:00:49,950
ហើយចែករំលែកភាពរីករាយ ដល់មនុស្សជុំវិញខ្លួន។

20
00:00:50,000 --> 00:00:52,450
ស្នេហា និងគ្រួសារថ្ងៃនេះ៖

21
00:00:52,500 --> 00:00:56,950
ជាថ្ងៃល្អសម្រាប់ទាក់ទងគ្នា ញ៉ាំអាហារជាមួយគ្នា

22
00:00:57,000 --> 00:00:59,950
និងបង្ហាញក្ដីស្រឡាញ់ដោយបើកចំហ។

23
00:01:00,000 --> 00:01:01,987
ការងារថ្ងៃនេះ៖ អតិថិជន

24
00:01:02,037 --> 00:01:05,506
និងមិត្តរួមការងារ ឆ្លើយតបល្អចំពោះអ្នក។

25
00:01:05,556 --> 00:01:08,006
ជាថ្ងៃល្អសម្រាប់បង្ហាញគំនិត

26

និងបិទកិច្ចព្រមព្រៀង។

27
00:01:10,000 --> 00:01:14,293
លុយកាក់ថ្ងៃនេះ៖ ជាថ្ងៃល្អសម្រាប់ចំណូលនឹងនរ។

28
00:01:14,343 --> 00:01:18,031
សូមសន្សំប្រាក់ចំណូលថ្ងៃនេះមួយចំណែកតូច

29
00:01:18,081 --> 00:01:19,950
មុននឹងចាយលើខ្លួនឯង។

30
00:01:20,000 --> 00:01:22,941
ពណ៌សំណាងរបស់អ្នកថ្ងៃនេះ គឺពណ៌ត្នោត។

31
00:01:22,991 --> 00:01:26,275
សូមស្លៀកពាក់ពណ៌ត្នោត ឬយករបស់ពណ៌ត្នោតមួយ

32
00:01:26,325 --> 00:01:29,950
ជាប់ខ្លួន ដើម្បីរក្សាសំណាងល្អ ឲ្យនៅជិតអ្នក។

33
00:01:30,000 --> 00:01:32,314
ពេលល្អបំផុតរបស់អ្នកថ្ងៃនេះ

34
00:01:32,364 --> 00:01:34,768
គឺម៉ោង 1 ដល់ម៉ោង 3 ទៀបភ្លឺ។

35
00:01:34,818 --> 00:01:37,405
ជាពេលល្អ សម្រាប់ផែនការសំខាន់ៗ

36
00:01:37,455 --> 00:01:39,950
ការជួបជុំ ឬការចាប់ផ្ដើមថ្មី។

37

ទិសសំណាងរបស់អ្នកថ្ងៃនេះ គឺទិសឦសាន។

38
00:01:42,881 --> 00:01:46,475
ពេលចាប់ផ្ដើមធ្វើអ្វីថ្មី សូមបែរមុខទៅទិសឦសាន

39
00:01:46,525 --> 00:01:49,950
ហើយបោះជំហានទៅមុខ ដោយចិត្តស្ងប់ និងរីករាយ។

40
00:01:50,000 --> 00:01:53,677
សម្រាប់លេខសំណាង៖ លេខ 7 លេខ 88 និងលេខ 582។

41
00:01:53,727 --> 00:01:57,314
សូមសរសេរលេខសំណាងរបស់អ្នក នៅក្នុងមតិយោបល់

42
00:01:57,364 --> 00:01:59,950
ហើយតោះនិយាយម្ដងទៀត៖ 7 88 582!

43
00:02:00,000 --> 00:02:03,047
ថ្ងៃសំណាងប្រចាំសប្ដាហ៍ របស់ឆ្នាំរកា

44
00:02:03,097 --> 00:02:05,614
គឺថ្ងៃព្រហស្បតិ៍ និងថ្ងៃសៅរ៍។

45
00:02:05,664 --> 00:02:07,295
សូមចងចាំថ្ងៃទាំងនេះ

46

សម្រាប់ផែនការសំខាន់ៗ របស់អ្នក។

47
00:02:10,000 --> 00:02:12,622
សូមរំលឹកបន្តិច៖ អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំរកា

48
00:02:12,672 --> 00:02:15,984
ជួនកាលថ្លែងយោបល់រិះគន់ខ្លាំង ឬត្រង់ពេក។

49
00:02:16,034 --> 00:02:18,053
ថ្ងៃនេះ សូមរក្សាតុល្យភាព

50
00:02:18,103 --> 00:02:19,950
និងស្ដាប់ចិត្តខ្លួនឯង។

51
00:02:20,000 --> 00:02:22,514
ថ្ងៃនេះ សូមប្រាថ្នាអ្វីមួយតូចៗ

52
00:02:22,564 --> 00:02:26,446
ហើយធ្វើរឿងល្អមួយ ដើម្បីឈានទៅរកក្ដីប្រាថ្នានោះ។

53
00:02:26,496 --> 00:02:29,950
ចិត្តដែលស្ងប់ នឹងជួយនាំសំណាងល្អ មករកអ្នក។

54
00:02:30,000 --> 00:02:31,306
មុនថ្ងៃនេះបញ្ចប់

55
00:02:31,356 --> 00:02:34,526
សូមចំណាយពេលស្ងាត់មួយភ្លែត ដើម្បីអរគុណ។

56
00:02:34,576 --> 00:02:37,662
អរគុណមនុស្សដែលជួយអ្នក ហើយអរគុណខ្លួនឯង

57
00:02:37,712 --> 00:02:39,950
ដែលបានព្យាយាមអស់ពីសមត្ថភាព។

58
00:02:40,000 --> 00:02:41,630
សូមចងចាំសំណាងថ្ងៃនេះ។

59
00:02:41,680 --> 00:02:43,150
ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌ត្នោត។

60
00:02:43,200 --> 00:02:45,790
ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោង 1 ដល់ម៉ោង 3 ទៀបភ្លឺ។

61
00:02:45,840 --> 00:02:47,230
ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសឦសាន។

62
00:02:47,280 --> 00:02:49,950
លេខសំណាង៖ លេខ 7 លេខ 88 និងលេខ 582។

63
00:02:50,000 --> 00:02:52,258
នេះគឺជាជោគជតារាសីចិន សម្រាប់ថ្ងៃនេះ។

64
00:02:52,308 --> 00:02:54,373
អ្នកអាចមើលរាសីទាំង 12 និងលេខសំណាង

65
00:02:54,423 --> 00:02:56,232
នៅលើគេហទំព័រ mybirthsign.com។

66
00:02:56,282 --> 00:02:58,540
សូមតាមដានយើង សម្រាប់ជោគជតាថ្ងៃស្អែក។

67
00:02:58,590 --> 00:02:59,950
សូមឲ្យអ្នកមានសំណាងល្អ!

### Subtitles – English (2026-11-19-180s.en.srt)

1
00:00:00,000 --> 00:00:02,807

00:00:02,857 --> 00:00:04,861
Here's your lucky color

3
00:00:04,911 --> 00:00:07,718
best time, and numbers for today

4

plus one gentle reminder.

5
00:00:10,000 --> 00:00:12,341
Today, Thursday, 19 November 2026

6
00:00:12,391 --> 00:00:13,573
is a Rooster day.

7
00:00:13,623 --> 00:00:16,109
It is a Great Day for everyone born

8
00:00:16,159 --> 00:00:18,066
in the Year of the Rooster.

9
00:00:18,116 --> 00:00:19,950
This is your day to shine!

10
00:00:20,000 --> 00:00:23,450

11

honesty, confidence, and a bright new day.

12
00:00:27,000 --> 00:00:29,950
Today, this good energy is with you.

13
00:00:30,000 --> 00:00:32,847
People born in the Year of the Rooster are

14
00:00:32,897 --> 00:00:35,536
observant, hard-working, and confident.

15
00:00:35,586 --> 00:00:37,467
Roosters are detail-oriented

16
00:00:37,517 --> 00:00:39,950
and take pride in doing things well.

17
00:00:40,000 --> 00:00:41,866
Born in the Year of the Rooster?

18
00:00:41,916 --> 00:00:43,423
Your luck is strong today.

19
00:00:43,473 --> 00:00:45,399
Stay proud, confident and bright.

20
00:00:45,449 --> 00:00:47,435
Say yes to good chances, and share

21
00:00:47,485 --> 00:00:49,950
your happiness with the people around you.

22
00:00:50,000 --> 00:00:52,394
Love and family today:

23
00:00:52,444 --> 00:00:56,506
A good day to reach out, share a meal

24
00:00:56,556 --> 00:00:59,950
and show your affection openly.

25
00:01:00,000 --> 00:01:01,969
Work today: Customers

26
00:01:02,019 --> 00:01:05,238
and coworkers respond well to you.

27
00:01:05,288 --> 00:01:08,315
A good day to present your ideas

28
00:01:08,365 --> 00:01:09,950

29
00:01:10,000 --> 00:01:12,611

30
00:01:12,661 --> 00:01:14,262
for steady income.

31
00:01:14,312 --> 00:01:16,831
Save a small part of today's

32
00:01:16,881 --> 00:01:19,950

33
00:01:20,000 --> 00:01:22,807
Your lucky color today is brown.

34
00:01:22,857 --> 00:01:24,593
Wear something brown

35
00:01:24,643 --> 00:01:27,807
or carry a small brown item with you

36
00:01:27,857 --> 00:01:29,950
to keep good luck close.

37
00:01:30,000 --> 00:01:31,603
Your best time today

38
00:01:31,653 --> 00:01:34,578
is from 1 to 3 in the early morning.

39
00:01:34,628 --> 00:01:37,140
It is a good time for important

## Voice-over – long YouTube video
The same lines as the voice in each scene above, all together, for recording the voice yourself or with text-to-speech (then copy the scenes with plain "Copy", so Flow does not add a voice too).

### English
Scene 1 (0:00–0:10)
Three signs are lucky today, and one should take it easy. Where does your sign stand?

Scene 2 (0:10–0:20)
Today, Thursday, 19 November 2026, is a Rooster day. It is a Great Day for everyone born in the Year of the Rooster. This is your day to shine!

Scene 3 (0:20–0:30)
Herald of the dawn, the Rooster symbolizes honesty, confidence, and a bright new day. Today, this good energy is with you.

Scene 4 (0:30–0:40)
Today's luckiest signs are the Rooster, Ox and Snake. Rabbit, take it easy today. Find your own sign in the chapters below.

Scene 5 (0:40–0:50)
Year of the Rat. Today is an ordinary, steady day for you. Your lucky color is gold, and your best time is from 7 to 9 in the morning.

Scene 6 (0:50–1:00)
Love: A calm day for relationships. Money: Everyday spending is best kept simple. Lucky direction: southeast.

Scene 7 (1:00–1:10)
Year of the Ox. Today is a good day for you. Your lucky color is green, and your best time is from 9 to 11 in the morning.

Scene 8 (1:10–1:20)
Love: Spend a little quality time with someone close. Lucky direction: southeast. Lucky numbers: 12 and 414.

Scene 9 (1:20–1:30)
Year of the Tiger. Today is an ordinary, steady day for you. Your lucky color is blue, and your best time is from 11 in the morning to 1 in the afternoon.

Scene 10 (1:30–1:40)
Love: A calm day for relationships. Lucky direction: south.

Scene 11 (1:40–1:50)
Year of the Rabbit. Today is a quiet day for you. Your lucky color is red, and your best time is from 1 to 3 in the afternoon.

Scene 12 (1:50–2:00)

## Story video – YouTube (about 5 minutes)
"The Golden Rooster and the Runaway Golden Kite" (មាន់មាស និងខ្លែងមាសដែលហោះបាត់): an original fairy tale (5:00) starring today's luckiest animal. A new story every day: today the golden rooster and Dara the little bird must bring the golden kite home before the full-moon festival. Today's lucky color, direction, best time, lucky numbers and lucky days are part of the story. Make the picture of Dara once (prompt below) and add it, Chanda and the Rooster picture as ingredients in Flow. Chanda opens the story on camera and narrates the rest off screen; Dara and the animals never speak (their words are told by Chanda). 30 clips of 10 seconds, in order 1 to 30.

### Character prompt – Dara the little bird (make once, keep it)
Create a photorealistic image of Dara, a tiny, lifelike magical blue-and-gold bird with bright, expressive eyes, soft round feathers and a little golden crest, as a small magical companion in a high-budget fantasy epic: realistic anatomy and textures with a soft warm magical glow, sitting on a glowing pink lotus by a still pond at night, a temple inspired by Angkor Wat and an enormous full moon behind, golden dust in the air. Cinematic lighting, beautiful depth of field, ultra-detailed. Square 1:1, full body visible, centered. No text, no letters, no watermark, no cartoon or plastic look.

### Topic and audience
An original animated bedtime story starring the Rooster, with today's lucky color, direction and numbers in the plot. For families, children and zodiac fans who enjoy gentle stories.

### Opening hook – 5 options
★ 1. Story question
   English: Can one little golden rooster bring the golden kite home before the full moon rises?
   Khmer: តើមាន់មាសតូចមួយ អាចនាំខ្លែងមាសត្រឡប់មកផ្ទះវិញ មុនព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់រះ បានទេ?
2. Mystery
   English: On the night of the full moon, something at the temple went wrong. Only one little hero could fix it.
   Khmer: នៅយប់ព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ មានរឿងមួយមិនប្រក្រតី នៅឯប្រាសាទ។ មានតែវីរបុរសតូចម្នាក់ប៉ុណ្ណោះ ដែលអាចជួយបាន។
3. Identity
   English: Born in the Year of the Rooster? This bedtime story is about you.
   Khmer: អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំរកាមែនទេ? រឿងនិទាននេះ គឺនិយាយអំពីអ្នក។
4. Challenge
   English: Three lucky treasures. One hard challenge. One golden rooster. Can it make it in time?
   Khmer: រតនសម្បត្តិសំណាងបី។ ឧបសគ្គមួយ។ មាន់មាសមួយ។ តើវាទាន់ពេលឬទេ?
5. Practical
   English: Today's lucky color, direction, and numbers, told as a magical story.
   Khmer: ពណ៌ ទិស និងលេខសំណាងថ្ងៃនេះ ក្នុងរឿងនិទានដ៏អស្ចារ្យមួយ។
★ = used to open this video (the hook changes from day to day). Why: Opens on the story's real question, which the ending answers, and the hero is today's luckiest animal.

### Thumbnail prompt – YouTube (16:9)
Make the picture in an image AI (for example Gemini or Flow) with Chanda's picture and the saved zodiac characters (named after their animals) added as references. Upload size: 16:9, at least 640 pixels wide (3840 x 2160 is best), JPG or PNG, under 2 MB if you upload from your phone.

Eye-catching YouTube thumbnail, horizontal 16:9, 3840 x 2160. Photorealistic still from a high-budget fantasy epic blending Khmer royal heritage with a Chinese historical fantasy drama: real human faces with natural skin, lifelike zodiac animals in engraved ceremonial gold armor with real metallic reflections, very expressive faces and eyes. Background: a glowing Khmer temple inspired by Angkor Wat under an enormous full moon and a deep indigo starry sky, lotus pool, golden particles. Bright, high-contrast, rich colors (imperial gold, deep navy, ruby red) that stand out on a small phone screen; strong golden rim light separating the characters from the background; slightly blurred background so the faces pop. Big, simple composition with only two or three subjects. Keep the bottom-right corner free (YouTube puts the video length there). No watermark, no logo, no YouTube buttons or arrows, no cartoon or plastic look. @Rooster (the Rooster guardian with their rooster) big in the foreground with an amazed, wide-eyed face, holding a glowing golden storybook that lights up its face; behind it, a glowing temple door and a path of lanterns lead into the night. The text sits across the top of the frame in the dark sky, never over a face.

### Audio design
Music: Gentle magical background music with soft chimes and a light Asian flute. No other voices: the animals do not speak. Keep it well below the voice (about 20 dB quieter) so every word is clear. ការអានរាសីចិនតាមប្រពៃណី សម្រាប់ការកម្សាន្ត និងការឆ្លុះបញ្ចាំងខ្លួនប៉ុណ្ណោះ មិនមែនជាការព្យាករណ៍វិទ្យាសាស្ត្រទេ។

#ជោគជតារាសី #រាសីប្រចាំថ្ងៃ #ឆ្នាំរកា #ផ្កាយកំណើត #រឿងនិទាន

### Scene 1 – The hook – Chanda on camera (0:00–0:10)
@Chanda, a beautiful Cambodian woman in her early twenties with a warm, kind and confident presence: oval face, soft defined cheekbones, warm amber-brown almond eyes, natural brows, rosy cheeks, natural skin with real texture and refined natural makeup, glossy black hair swept into a low side bun on her right side held by an elaborate golden lotus hairpin with fine dangling gold chains, delicate gold flower earrings, a fitted cream-and-gold Khmer silk brocade blouse with a high mandarin collar and long sleeves, a large round gold medallion on a chain engraved with all twelve zodiac animals, an ornate gold belt with a single ruby-red gemstone at the buckle, a deep navy silk sampot to the ankles with a vertical gold-embroidered front panel and a wide band of Khmer gold patterns at the hem, and gold bangles on both wrists, stands on the moonlit temple terrace beside the lotus pond, facing the camera, smiling warmly and speaking directly to the viewer with natural lip movements and small, friendly hand gestures, holding a glowing golden storybook as if about to begin a bedtime story. Behind her, @Rooster (the Rooster guardian with their rooster) glows softly on its lotus pedestal; the zodiac characters look around curiously but do not speak. Medium shot, gentle push-in toward her face. Chanda is the saved Flow character @Chanda; the rooster is the saved Flow character @Rooster. She speaks with heartfelt enthusiasm: a joyful smile, bright eyes and lively, natural hand gestures, as if she can't wait to tell the viewer something special. Natural, believable acting with real micro-expressions, never exaggerated or theatrical.
English voice: Audio: Chanda's voice, identical in every clip of this series (the same woman, same timbre, same pitch, same accent, same speed, same energy): a young Cambodian woman in her mid-twenties speaking clear, natural English with a neutral accent, medium-high pitch, a soft, warm and bright tone, a gentle smile in her voice, the same calm, cheerful energy from the first word to the last (never louder, faster, deeper, older or more dramatic), a steady pace of about 150 words per minute, even volume, recorded close to a good studio microphone in a quiet room with no echo or reverb; no whispering, no singing, no other voices. Chanda is on camera and speaks to the viewer with natural lip movements that match her words. She says: "Can one little golden rooster bring the golden kite home before the full moon rises? Once upon a time, beside an ancient temple in Cambodia, our story begins." Gentle magical background music with soft chimes and a light Asian flute. No other voices: the animals do not speak.

## Voice-over – story video
The same lines as the voice in each scene above, all together, for recording the voice yourself or with text-to-speech (then copy the scenes with plain "Copy", so Flow does not add a voice too).

### English
Scene 1 (0:00–0:10)
Can one little golden rooster bring the golden kite home before the full moon rises? Once upon a time, beside an ancient temple in Cambodia, our story begins.

Scene 2 (0:10–0:20)
But this morning, a strong wind snapped its string, and the golden kite flew far away. Without it, the festival could not begin.

Scene 3 (0:20–0:30)
In the middle of the pond, a golden lotus opened, and out came a little golden rooster. Today was a Rooster day, and the rooster felt something special.

Scene 4 (0:30–0:40)
A little bird named Dara flew down. "Little Rooster," she said, "only you can bring the golden kite home. Today is your lucky day!"

Scene 5 (0:40–0:50)
Herald of the dawn, the Rooster symbolizes honesty, confidence, and a bright new day. Dara smiled. That was exactly why the moon had chosen it.

Scene 6 (0:50–1:00)
To bring the golden kite home, the little hero needed three lucky treasures: its lucky color, its lucky path, and its lucky numbers.

Scene 7 (1:00–1:10)
"Let's go!" said the rooster, and it set off on its adventure, with Dara right by its side.

Scene 8 (1:10–1:20)
People born in the Year of the Rooster are observant, hard-working, and confident. Roosters are detail-oriented and take pride in doing things well.

Scene 9 (1:20–1:30)
The first treasure was waiting in a garden of a thousand lanterns. Only one shone brightest. It was brown, the rooster's lucky color today.

Scene 10 (1:30–1:40)
The rooster tied a brown ribbon around its neck. "Wear your lucky color," said Dara, "and good luck stays close to you."

Scene 11 (1:40–1:50)
Next, they came to a crossroads with many paths. One path began to glow. It pointed to the northeast, the rooster's lucky direction.

Scene 12 (1:50–2:00)
But on the path stood an old, shaky bridge. The rooster stopped. Sometimes, people born in the Year of the Rooster can be overly critical or blunt with others.

Scene 13 (2:00–2:10)
Dara came close and smiled. "Breathe slowly," she said. "Trust your heart. You are stronger than you think."

Scene 14 (2:10–2:20)
Step by step, the rooster crossed the bridge. With every step, the planks glowed gold. On the other side, it laughed with joy.

Scene 15 (2:20–2:30)
The little hero learned that its worries were small, and its good heart was big. Balance and courage always help us grow.

Scene 16 (2:30–2:40)
At last, they reached a great stone door in the temple. Carved above it were bells, stairs, and a riddle about lucky numbers.

Scene 17 (2:40–2:50)
"The door opens only at your best time," said Dara. For the Rooster today, that is from 1 to 3 in the early morning. They waited patiently.

Scene 18 (2:50–3:00)
When the time came, the rooster rang the golden bells 7 times, its first lucky number. Ding! The door began to shine.

Scene 19 (3:00–3:10)
The door opened, and a stairway of light appeared. The rooster climbed 88 glowing steps, its second lucky number, up to the top of the tower.

Scene 20 (3:10–3:20)
At the top of the tower, they found the golden kite, safe and shining, caught on a stone lotus. Hooray!

Scene 21 (3:20–3:30)
Around the kite, seven little lights twinkled, one for each day of the week. Thursday and Saturday shone brightest, the Rooster's lucky days.

Scene 22 (3:30–3:40)
Time was running out. Holding the kite carefully, the little hero hurried home, with Dara right beside it.

Scene 23 (3:40–3:50)
Back at the pond, everyone was waiting. The night was dark, and the full moon was rising. Was it too late?

Scene 24 (3:50–4:00)
The rooster tied the kite to its pole and let it fly. For a moment, it wobbled. Then, whoosh! It soared up high, shining gold!

Scene 25 (4:00–4:10)
The golden kite shone in the sky again. Lanterns rose into the sky, and the full-moon festival began. Everyone cheered for the little golden hero!

Scene 26 (4:10–4:20)
The full moon shone down warmly, as if to say thank you. The rooster felt peaceful and proud. It had done its very best.

Scene 27 (4:20–4:30)
If you were born in the Year of the Rooster, today is your day too. Like our hero, be brave, be kind, and follow your good heart.

Scene 28 (4:30–4:40)
Let's remember today's luck. Lucky color: brown. Best time: from 1 to 3 in the early morning. Lucky direction: northeast. Lucky numbers: 7, 88, and 582.

Scene 29 (4:40–4:50)
And so, under the bright full moon, the little golden hero and Dara fell fast asleep, dreaming of tomorrow.

Scene 30 (4:50–5:00)
That's your Chinese zodiac fortune for today. See all twelve signs and your lucky numbers at mybirthsign dot com. Follow us for tomorrow's fortune. Have a lucky day!

ប៉ុន្តែព្រឹកនេះ ខ្យល់បក់ខ្លាំង ធ្វើឲ្យខ្សែដាច់ ហើយខ្លែងមាសក៏ហោះទៅឆ្ងាយ។ បើគ្មានវា ពិធីបុណ្យមិនអាចចាប់ផ្ដើមបានទេ។

Scene 3 (0:20–0:30)
នៅកណ្ដាលស្រះ ផ្កាឈូកមាសមួយបានរីក ហើយមានមាន់មាសតូចមួយ ចេញមក។ ថ្ងៃនេះជាថ្ងៃឆ្នាំរកា ហើយមាន់មានអារម្មណ៍ពិសេសណាស់។

មាន់ គឺជាអ្នកប្រកាសពេលព្រឹកព្រលឹម។ ឆ្នាំរកាតំណាងឲ្យភាពស្មោះត្រង់ ទំនុកចិត្ត និងថ្ងៃថ្មីដ៏ភ្លឺស្វាង។ តារាញញឹម។ នេះហើយ ជាហេតុដែលព្រះចន្ទ បានជ្រើសរើសវា។

ដើម្បីនាំខ្លែងមាសត្រឡប់មកផ្ទះវិញ វីរបុរសតូច ត្រូវការរតនសម្បត្តិសំណាងបី៖ ពណ៌សំណាង ផ្លូវសំណាង និងលេខសំណាង។

Scene 16 (2:30–2:40)

Scene 17 (2:40–2:50)

Scene 18 (2:50–3:00)

Scene 19 (3:00–3:10)
ទ្វារបើក ហើយមានជណ្ដើរពន្លឺមួយ លេចឡើង។ មាន់ឡើងជណ្ដើរភ្លឺ ចំនួនប៉ែតសិបប្រាំបី កាំ ដែលជាលេខសំណាងទីពីររបស់វា រហូតដល់កំពូលប្រាង្គ។

Scene 20 (3:10–3:20)

Scene 21 (3:20–3:30)

Scene 22 (3:30–3:40)
ពេលវេលាជិតអស់ហើយ។ វីរបុរសតូច កាន់ខ្លែងយ៉ាងប្រុងប្រយ័ត្ន ហើយប្រញាប់ត្រឡប់ទៅផ្ទះវិញ ដោយមានតារា នៅក្បែរ។

Scene 23 (3:40–3:50)
នៅឯស្រះវិញ អ្នកទាំងអស់គ្នា កំពុងរង់ចាំ។ យប់ងងឹត ហើយព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ កំពុងរះ។ តើយឺតពេលហើយឬ?

Scene 24 (3:50–4:00)
មាន់ចងខ្លែងទៅនឹងបង្គោល ហើយបង្ហោះវា។ មួយសន្ទុះ វាយោលទៅមក។ បន្ទាប់មក វឺត! វាហោះឡើងខ្ពស់ ភ្លឺពណ៌មាស!

Scene 25 (4:00–4:10)
ខ្លែងមាស ភ្លឺលើមេឃម្ដងទៀត។ គោមហោះឡើងលើមេឃ ហើយពិធីបុណ្យព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ ក៏ចាប់ផ្ដើម។ អ្នកទាំងអស់គ្នា អបអរសាទរវីរបុរសមាសតូច!

Scene 26 (4:10–4:20)
ព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ បញ្ចាំងពន្លឺកក់ក្ដៅចុះមក ហាក់ដូចជាពោលពាក្យអរគុណ។ មាន់មានអារម្មណ៍ស្ងប់ និងមានមោទនភាព។ វាបានព្យាយាមអស់ពីសមត្ថភាព។

Scene 27 (4:20–4:30)
បើអ្នកកើតឆ្នាំរកា ថ្ងៃនេះ ក៏ជាថ្ងៃរបស់អ្នកដែរ។ ដូចវីរបុរសតូចរបស់យើង សូមក្លាហាន មានចិត្តល្អ ហើយធ្វើតាមចិត្តល្អរបស់អ្នក។

Scene 28 (4:30–4:40)
សូមចងចាំសំណាងថ្ងៃនេះ។ ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌ត្នោត។ ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោងមួយ ដល់ម៉ោងបី ទៀបភ្លឺ។ ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសឦសាន។ លេខសំណាង៖ លេខប្រាំពីរ លេខប៉ែតសិបប្រាំបី និងលេខប្រាំរយប៉ែតសិបពីរ។

Scene 29 (4:40–4:50)
ហើយដូច្នេះ នៅក្រោមពន្លឺព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ វីរបុរសមាសតូច និងតារា ក៏លង់លក់ ដោយសុបិនអំពីថ្ងៃស្អែក។

Scene 30 (4:50–5:00)
នេះគឺជាជោគជតារាសីចិន សម្រាប់ថ្ងៃនេះ។ អ្នកអាចមើលរាសីទាំងដប់ពីរ និងលេខសំណាង នៅលើគេហទំព័រ mybirthsign.com។ សូមតាមដានយើង សម្រាប់ជោគជតាថ្ងៃស្អែក។ សូមឲ្យអ្នកមានសំណាងល្អ!

ជាប់នៅលើផ្កាឈូកថ្ម។

78
00:03:18,700 --> 00:03:19,950
អស្ចារ្យណាស់!

79
00:03:20,000 --> 00:03:22,295
នៅជុំវិញខ្លែង មានពន្លឺតូចៗប្រាំពីរ

80
00:03:22,345 --> 00:03:23,329
ភ្លឺភ្លឹបភ្លែតៗ

81
00:03:23,379 --> 00:03:25,605
មួយសម្រាប់ថ្ងៃនីមួយៗក្នុងសប្ដាហ៍។

82
00:03:25,655 --> 00:03:28,088
ថ្ងៃព្រហស្បតិ៍ និងថ្ងៃសៅរ៍ ភ្លឺជាងគេ

83
00:03:28,138 --> 00:03:29,950
ដែលជាថ្ងៃសំណាងរបស់ឆ្នាំរកា។

84
00:03:30,000 --> 00:03:31,667
ពេលវេលាជិតអស់ហើយ។

85
00:03:31,717 --> 00:03:35,405
វីរបុរសតូច កាន់ខ្លែងយ៉ាងប្រុងប្រយ័ត្ន

86
00:03:35,455 --> 00:03:39,950
ហើយប្រញាប់ត្រឡប់ទៅផ្ទះវិញ ដោយមានតារា នៅក្បែរ។

87
00:03:40,000 --> 00:03:44,485
នៅឯស្រះវិញ អ្នកទាំងអស់គ្នា កំពុងរង់ចាំ។

88
00:03:44,535 --> 00:03:48,438
យប់ងងឹត ហើយព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ កំពុងរះ។

89
00:03:48,488 --> 00:03:49,950
តើយឺតពេលហើយឬ?

90
00:03:50,000 --> 00:03:53,673
មាន់ចងខ្លែងទៅនឹងបង្គោល ហើយបង្ហោះវា។

91
00:03:53,723 --> 00:03:55,801
មួយសន្ទុះ វាយោលទៅមក។

92
00:03:55,851 --> 00:03:57,290
បន្ទាប់មក វឺត!

93
00:03:57,340 --> 00:03:59,950
វាហោះឡើងខ្ពស់ ភ្លឺពណ៌មាស!

94
00:04:00,000 --> 00:04:02,172
ខ្លែងមាស ភ្លឺលើមេឃម្ដងទៀត។

95
00:04:02,222 --> 00:04:03,369
គោមហោះឡើងលើមេឃ

96
00:04:03,419 --> 00:04:06,702
ហើយពិធីបុណ្យព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ ក៏ចាប់ផ្ដើម។

97
00:04:06,752 --> 00:04:09,950
អ្នកទាំងអស់គ្នា អបអរសាទរវីរបុរសមាសតូច!

98
00:04:10,000 --> 00:04:13,121
ព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ បញ្ចាំងពន្លឺកក់ក្ដៅចុះមក

99
00:04:13,171 --> 00:04:14,991
ហាក់ដូចជាពោលពាក្យអរគុណ។

100
00:04:15,041 --> 00:04:17,836
មាន់មានអារម្មណ៍ស្ងប់ និងមានមោទនភាព។

101
00:04:17,886 --> 00:04:19,950
វាបានព្យាយាមអស់ពីសមត្ថភាព។

102
00:04:20,000 --> 00:04:23,916
បើអ្នកកើតឆ្នាំរកា ថ្ងៃនេះ ក៏ជាថ្ងៃរបស់អ្នកដែរ។

103
00:04:23,966 --> 00:04:26,588
ដូចវីរបុរសតូចរបស់យើង សូមក្លាហាន

104
00:04:26,638 --> 00:04:29,950
មានចិត្តល្អ ហើយធ្វើតាមចិត្តល្អរបស់អ្នក។

105
00:04:30,000 --> 00:04:31,630
សូមចងចាំសំណាងថ្ងៃនេះ។

106
00:04:31,680 --> 00:04:33,150
ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌ត្នោត។

107
00:04:33,200 --> 00:04:35,790
ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោង 1 ដល់ម៉ោង 3 ទៀបភ្លឺ។

108
00:04:35,840 --> 00:04:37,230
ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសឦសាន។

109
00:04:37,280 --> 00:04:39,950
លេខសំណាង៖ លេខ 7 លេខ 88 និងលេខ 582។

110
00:04:40,000 --> 00:04:44,107
ហើយដូច្នេះ នៅក្រោមពន្លឺព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់

111
00:04:44,157 --> 00:04:47,478
វីរបុរសមាសតូច និងតារា ក៏លង់លក់

112
00:04:47,528 --> 00:04:49,950
ដោយសុបិនអំពីថ្ងៃស្អែក។

113
00:04:50,000 --> 00:04:52,258
នេះគឺជាជោគជតារាសីចិន សម្រាប់ថ្ងៃនេះ។

114
00:04:52,308 --> 00:04:54,373
អ្នកអាចមើលរាសីទាំង 12 និងលេខសំណាង

115
00:04:54,423 --> 00:04:56,232
នៅលើគេហទំព័រ mybirthsign.com។

116
00:04:56,282 --> 00:04:58,540
សូមតាមដានយើង សម្រាប់ជោគជតាថ្ងៃស្អែក។

117
00:04:58,590 --> 00:04:59,950
សូមឲ្យអ្នកមានសំណាងល្អ!
```

### Subtitles – English (2026-11-19-story.en.srt)
```
1
00:00:00,000 --> 00:00:01,833
Can one little golden rooster

2
00:00:01,883 --> 00:00:03,521
bring the golden kite home

3
00:00:03,571 --> 00:00:05,275
before the full moon rises?

4
00:00:05,325 --> 00:00:08,002
Once upon a time, beside an ancient temple

5
00:00:08,052 --> 00:00:09,950
in Cambodia, our story begins.

6
00:00:10,000 --> 00:00:11,251
But this morning

7
00:00:11,301 --> 00:00:13,852
a strong wind snapped its string

8
00:00:13,902 --> 00:00:16,617
and the golden kite flew far away.

9
00:00:16,667 --> 00:00:19,950
Without it, the festival could not begin.

10
00:00:20,000 --> 00:00:21,674
In the middle of the pond

11
00:00:21,724 --> 00:00:23,122
a golden lotus opened

12
00:00:23,172 --> 00:00:25,674
and out came a little golden rooster.

13
00:00:25,724 --> 00:00:27,260
Today was a Rooster day

14
00:00:27,310 --> 00:00:29,950
and the rooster felt something special.

15
00:00:30,000 --> 00:00:32,706
A little bird named Dara flew down.

16
00:00:32,756 --> 00:00:34,753
"Little Rooster," she said

17
00:00:34,803 --> 00:00:37,981
"only you can bring the golden kite home.

18
00:00:38,031 --> 00:00:39,950
Today is your lucky day!"

19
00:00:40,000 --> 00:00:42,972
Herald of the dawn, the Rooster symbolizes

20
00:00:43,022 --> 00:00:45,993
honesty, confidence, and a bright new day.

21
00:00:46,043 --> 00:00:46,856
Dara smiled.

22
00:00:46,906 --> 00:00:48,295
That was exactly why

23
00:00:48,345 --> 00:00:49,950
the moon had chosen it.

24
00:00:50,000 --> 00:00:52,233
To bring the golden kite home

25
00:00:52,283 --> 00:00:54,438
the little hero needed three

26
00:00:54,488 --> 00:00:56,958
lucky treasures: its lucky color

27
00:00:57,008 --> 00:00:59,950
its lucky path, and its lucky numbers.

28
00:01:00,000 --> 00:01:03,168
"Let's go!" said the rooster

29
00:01:03,218 --> 00:01:06,732
and it set off on its adventure

30
00:01:06,782 --> 00:01:09,950
with Dara right by its side.

31
00:01:10,000 --> 00:01:12,847
People born in the Year of the Rooster are

32
00:01:12,897 --> 00:01:15,536
observant, hard-working, and confident.

33
00:01:15,586 --> 00:01:17,467
Roosters are detail-oriented

34
00:01:17,517 --> 00:01:19,950
and take pride in doing things well.

35
00:01:20,000 --> 00:01:22,172
The first treasure was waiting

36
00:01:22,222 --> 00:01:24,765
in a garden of a thousand lanterns.

37
00:01:24,815 --> 00:01:26,617
Only one shone brightest.

38
00:01:26,667 --> 00:01:28,617
It was brown, the rooster's

39
00:01:28,667 --> 00:01:29,950
lucky color today.

40
00:01:30,000 --> 00:01:32,019
The rooster tied a brown

41
00:01:32,069 --> 00:01:34,002
ribbon around its neck.

42
00:01:34,052 --> 00:01:36,933
"Wear your lucky color," said Dara

43
00:01:36,983 --> 00:01:39,950
"and good luck stays close to you."

44
00:01:40,000 --> 00:01:42,391
Next, they came to a crossroads

45
00:01:42,441 --> 00:01:43,651
with many paths.

46
00:01:43,701 --> 00:01:45,462
One path began to glow.

47
00:01:45,512 --> 00:01:47,588
It pointed to the northeast

48
00:01:47,638 --> 00:01:49,950
the rooster's lucky direction.

49
00:01:50,000 --> 00:01:51,314
But on the path stood

50
00:01:51,364 --> 00:01:52,677
an old, shaky bridge.

51
00:01:52,727 --> 00:01:53,976
The rooster stopped.

52
00:01:54,026 --> 00:01:56,184
Sometimes, people born in the Year

53
00:01:56,234 --> 00:01:58,586
of the Rooster can be overly critical

54
00:01:58,636 --> 00:01:59,950
or blunt with others.

55
00:02:00,000 --> 00:02:02,521
Dara came close and smiled.

56
00:02:02,571 --> 00:02:05,093
"Breathe slowly," she said.

57
00:02:05,143 --> 00:02:06,807
"Trust your heart.

58
00:02:06,857 --> 00:02:09,950
You are stronger than you think."

59
00:02:10,000 --> 00:02:11,983
Step by step, the rooster

60
00:02:12,033 --> 00:02:13,527
crossed the bridge.

61
00:02:13,577 --> 00:02:16,779
With every step, the planks glowed gold.

62
00:02:16,829 --> 00:02:19,950
On the other side, it laughed with joy.

63
00:02:20,000 --> 00:02:23,311
The little hero learned that its worries

64
00:02:23,361 --> 00:02:26,589
were small, and its good heart was big.

65
00:02:26,639 --> 00:02:29,950
Balance and courage always help us grow.

66
00:02:30,000 --> 00:02:32,347
At last, they reached a great

67
00:02:32,397 --> 00:02:34,413
stone door in the temple.

68
00:02:34,463 --> 00:02:36,562
Carved above it were bells

69
00:02:36,612 --> 00:02:39,950
stairs, and a riddle about lucky numbers.

70
00:02:40,000 --> 00:02:41,443
"The door opens only

71
00:02:41,493 --> 00:02:43,681
at your best time," said Dara.

72
00:02:43,731 --> 00:02:45,249
For the Rooster today

73
00:02:45,299 --> 00:02:48,308
that is from 1 to 3 in the early morning.

74
00:02:48,358 --> 00:02:49,950
They waited patiently.

75
00:02:50,000 --> 00:02:51,475
When the time came

76
00:02:51,525 --> 00:02:54,950
the rooster rang the golden bells 7 times

77
00:02:55,000 --> 00:02:56,899
its first lucky number.

78
00:02:56,949 --> 00:02:57,916
Ding!

79
00:02:57,966 --> 00:02:59,950
The door began to shine.

80
00:03:00,000 --> 00:03:01,069
The door opened

81
00:03:01,119 --> 00:03:03,532
and a stairway of light appeared.

82
00:03:03,582 --> 00:03:06,219
The rooster climbed 88 glowing steps

83
00:03:06,269 --> 00:03:07,935
its second lucky number

84
00:03:07,985 --> 00:03:09,950
up to the top of the tower.

85
00:03:10,000 --> 00:03:13,315
At the top of the tower, they found

86
00:03:13,365 --> 00:03:16,488
the golden kite, safe and shining

87
00:03:16,538 --> 00:03:18,796
caught on a stone lotus.

88
00:03:18,846 --> 00:03:19,950
Hooray!

89
00:03:20,000 --> 00:03:22,578
Around the kite, seven little lights

90
00:03:22,628 --> 00:03:25,424
twinkled, one for each day of the week.

91
00:03:25,474 --> 00:03:28,125
Thursday and Saturday shone brightest

92
00:03:28,175 --> 00:03:29,950
the Rooster's lucky days.

93
00:03:30,000 --> 00:03:32,029
Time was running out.

94
00:03:32,079 --> 00:03:34,603
Holding the kite carefully

95
00:03:34,653 --> 00:03:37,376
the little hero hurried home

96
00:03:37,426 --> 00:03:39,950
with Dara right beside it.

97
00:03:40,000 --> 00:03:43,774
Back at the pond, everyone was waiting.

98
00:03:43,824 --> 00:03:45,538
The night was dark

99
00:03:45,588 --> 00:03:48,381
and the full moon was rising.

100
00:03:48,431 --> 00:03:49,950
Was it too late?

101
00:03:50,000 --> 00:03:51,999
The rooster tied the kite

102
00:03:52,049 --> 00:03:54,212
to its pole and let it fly.

103
00:03:54,262 --> 00:03:56,261
For a moment, it wobbled.

104
00:03:56,311 --> 00:03:57,327
Then, whoosh!

105
00:03:57,377 --> 00:03:59,950
It soared up high, shining gold!

106
00:04:00,000 --> 00:04:02,716
The golden kite shone in the sky again.

107
00:04:02,766 --> 00:04:04,560
Lanterns rose into the sky

108
00:04:04,610 --> 00:04:06,900
and the full-moon festival began.

109
00:04:06,950 --> 00:04:08,035
Everyone cheered

110
00:04:08,085 --> 00:04:09,950
for the little golden hero!

111
00:04:10,000 --> 00:04:12,622
The full moon shone down warmly

112
00:04:12,672 --> 00:04:14,605
as if to say thank you.

113
00:04:14,655 --> 00:04:17,709
The rooster felt peaceful and proud.

114
00:04:17,759 --> 00:04:19,950
It had done its very best.

115
00:04:20,000 --> 00:04:22,190
If you were born in the Year

116
00:04:22,240 --> 00:04:25,230
of the Rooster, today is your day too.

117
00:04:25,280 --> 00:04:27,790
Like our hero, be brave, be kind

118
00:04:27,840 --> 00:04:29,950
and follow your good heart.

119
00:04:30,000 --> 00:04:31,829
Let's remember today's luck.

120
00:04:31,879 --> 00:04:33,104
Lucky color: brown.

121
00:04:33,154 --> 00:04:33,910
Best time:

122
00:04:33,960 --> 00:04:36,124
from 1 to 3 in the early morning.

123
00:04:36,174 --> 00:04:37,937
Lucky direction: northeast.

124
00:04:37,987 --> 00:04:39,950
Lucky numbers: 7, 88, and 582.

125
00:04:40,000 --> 00:04:43,219
And so, under the bright full moon

126
00:04:43,269 --> 00:04:46,681
the little golden hero and Dara fell

127
00:04:46,731 --> 00:04:49,950
fast asleep, dreaming of tomorrow.

128
00:04:50,000 --> 00:04:51,617
That's your Chinese zodiac

129
00:04:51,667 --> 00:04:52,771
fortune for today.

130
00:04:52,821 --> 00:04:54,053
See all twelve signs

131
00:04:54,103 --> 00:04:56,745
and your lucky numbers at mybirthsign.com.

132
00:04:56,795 --> 00:04:58,860
Follow us for tomorrow's fortune.

133
00:04:58,910 --> 00:04:59,950
Have a lucky day!
```

## Take-it-easy video – the unlucky sign, Rabbit (8 scenes, 80 seconds)
An 80-second video for the Rabbit, today's sign to take it easy (in the traditional day cycle the Rabbit clashes with the Rooster day): 8 clips of 10 seconds. Set the clip length in Flow to 10 seconds and add Chanda and the saved character @Rabbit as ingredients (the Rabbit is the only zodiac character in this video, from the first scene to the last). Chanda is on camera and speaks with lip-sync in scenes 1 and 8; in the other scenes she narrates off screen. The animals never speak or move their mouths: they only act. It is one continuous little story in one place (the rabbit hurries, wobbles, stops to breathe, a little paper crane of light keeps it company, it climbs the temple steps and looks out with hope), so make the clips in order and keep the same setting and light. No lucky numbers or colors in this video: it is about encouragement. Keep it gentle and realistic: a quiet day, not bad luck. For each scene, press "Copy + English voice" or "Copy + Khmer voice". Put the clips in order 1 to 8.

### Opening hook – 5 options
1. Stop the scroll
   English: Stop scrolling! Born in the Year of the Rabbit? Your luck isn't good today. But don't worry, watch to the end.
   Khmer: ឈប់! កុំអូសរំលង។ អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំថោះ រាសីអ្នកថ្ងៃនេះមិនល្អទេ។ តែកុំបារម្ភ សូមមើលឲ្យចប់។
2. Warning
   English: Year of the Rabbit, wait! Today is a clash day for you. Here's how to get through it calmly.
   Khmer: អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំថោះ ចាំបន្តិច! ថ្ងៃនេះជាថ្ងៃប៉ះឆុងសម្រាប់អ្នក។ មកមើលវិធីឆ្លងកាត់ថ្ងៃនេះដោយស្ងប់។
★ 3. Question
   English: Does everything feel a bit off today? If you were born in the Year of the Rabbit, here's why.
   Khmer: ថ្ងៃនេះ អ្វីៗហាក់ដូចជាមិនសូវស្រួលមែនទេ? បើអ្នកកើតឆ្នាំថោះ នេះជាមូលហេតុ។
4. Send it to a friend
   English: Know someone born in the Year of the Rabbit? Send this to them before they make a big decision today.
   Khmer: ស្គាល់អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំថោះទេ? ផ្ញើវីដេអូនេះទៅគាត់ មុនពេលគាត់សម្រេចចិត្តរឿងធំថ្ងៃនេះ។
5. Only one sign

### Scene 8 – Goodbye – Chanda on camera (1:10–1:20)
@Chanda, a beautiful Cambodian woman in her early twenties with a warm, kind and confident presence: oval face, soft defined cheekbones, warm amber-brown almond eyes, natural brows, rosy cheeks, natural skin with real texture and refined natural makeup, glossy black hair swept into a low side bun on her right side held by an elaborate golden lotus hairpin with fine dangling gold chains, delicate gold flower earrings, a fitted cream-and-gold Khmer silk brocade blouse with a high mandarin collar and long sleeves, a large round gold medallion on a chain engraved with all twelve zodiac animals, an ornate gold belt with a single ruby-red gemstone at the buckle, a deep navy silk sampot to the ankles with a vertical gold-embroidered front panel and a wide band of Khmer gold patterns at the hem, and gold bangles on both wrists, stands on the carved stone temple terrace above the moonlit lotus pond, facing the camera and speaking warmly to the viewer with natural lip movements and small, friendly hand gestures. As she speaks, Chanda gently holds out her hands to the little paper crane of light from the earlier scenes; it rises up between them and bursts softly into a shower of golden light and glowing lotus petals that drift down around them, wrapping the rabbit in a warm, gentle golden glow. The rabbit looks up at the light, relaxes and smiles, then waves goodbye together with Chanda, without speaking, its mouth closed. Lanterns float up over the pond behind them. No other animals. Medium shot, gentle push-in toward Chanda. Chanda is the saved Flow character @Chanda; the rabbit is the saved Flow character @Rabbit. ## Voice-over – take-it-easy video
The same lines as the voice in each scene above, all together, for recording the voice yourself or with text-to-speech (then copy the scenes with plain "Copy", so Flow does not add a voice too).

### English
Scene 1 (0:00–0:10)
Does everything feel a bit off today? If you were born in the Year of the Rabbit, here's why.

Scene 2 (0:10–0:20)
Today, 19 November, is a Rooster day, opposite the Rabbit in the zodiac circle. Some days don't go to plan.

Scene 3 (0:20–0:30)
And that's okay. Everyone has days like this. A quiet day is not a bad day. It is a day to breathe.

Scene 4 (0:30–0:40)
Be careful today: you can be overly cautious and avoid necessary confrontation. Pause before you react. Be kind to yourself.

Scene 5 (0:40–0:50)
Be gentle with the people you love, and with yourself. And lean on a friend you trust.

Scene 6 (0:50–1:00)
At work and with money, take small, careful steps. Finish one thing, then the next. Small steps still count.

Scene 7 (1:00–1:10)
Slow is still moving forward. Every quiet day makes you stronger, and brighter days are already on their way.

Scene 8 (1:10–1:20)
Rest well tonight. Tomorrow is a new day, and it could be yours. Follow MyBirthSign for your fortune every day!

## Long YouTube video – full fortune, 8:40 (mid-roll ads)
A full daily fortune for YouTube (8:40, 52 clips of 10 seconds), long enough for mid-roll ads. In order: the hook, the Great Day sign (Rooster) in depth, the two Good Day signs (Ox and Snake), the sign to take it easy (Rabbit) with its little story, the other eight signs, then a recap and goodbye. Add Chanda and the 12 saved characters as ingredients in Flow (@Rat, @Ox … @Pig; each part names its own character). Chanda is on camera and speaks with lip-sync in scenes 1, 4 and 52; in the others she narrates off screen. The animals never speak or move their mouths: they only act. Set the clip length to 10 seconds, make the clips in order 1 to 52, and paste the chapters into the YouTube description.

### YouTube chapters (paste into the description)
0:00 Introduction
0:40 Great Day: Rooster
2:40 Good Day: Ox
3:30 Good Day: Snake
4:20 Take it easy: Rabbit
5:20 All the other signs
8:00 Recap and goodbye

### Opening hook – 5 options
1. Every sign in one line
   English: One sign has a Great Day today, two have a Good Day, and one should slow down. Which one are you?
   Khmer: ថ្ងៃនេះ មានមួយឆ្នាំថ្ងៃល្អខ្លាំង ពីរឆ្នាំថ្ងៃល្អ និងមួយឆ្នាំគួរធ្វើអ្វីៗយឺតៗ។ តើអ្នកជាឆ្នាំណា?
2. Before a big decision
   English: Before you make any big decision today, watch this: your lucky color, your best time, and whether to slow down.
   Khmer: មុននឹងសម្រេចចិត្តរឿងធំថ្ងៃនេះ សូមមើលវីដេអូនេះ៖ ពណ៌សំណាង ពេលល្អ និងថាតើគួរធ្វើយឺតៗឬទេ។
3. Speak to the careful sign
   English: Born in the Year of the Rabbit? Watch this before you do anything big today. Everyone else, your luck is here too.
   Khmer: អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំថោះមែនទេ? សូមមើលវីដេអូនេះ មុនធ្វើរឿងធំថ្ងៃនេះ។ អ្នកផ្សេងទៀត សំណាងរបស់អ្នកក៏នៅទីនេះដែរ។
4. Name the winner
   English: Today the Rooster has its Great Day, and one sign should take it easy. Is it yours?
   Khmer: ថ្ងៃនេះ ឆ្នាំរកាមានថ្ងៃល្អខ្លាំង ហើយមានមួយឆ្នាំគួរប្រុងប្រយ័ត្ន។ តើជាឆ្នាំរបស់អ្នកឬទេ?
★ 5. Comment first
   English: Comment your sign now. By the end of this video, you'll know your lucky color, best time, and direction for today.

### Mid-roll ads
YouTube lets you add ad breaks in the middle of videos that are 8 minutes or longer; this one is 8:40. Put the breaks at chapter starts so they don't cut a sentence. Chapter starts after 2:00: 2:40, 3:30, 4:20, 5:20, 8:00. Two or three breaks are enough; too many make viewers leave.

## Voice-over – full fortune video
The same lines as the voice in each scene above, all together, for recording the voice yourself or with text-to-speech (then copy the scenes with plain "Copy", so Flow does not add a voice too).

### English
Scene 1 (0:00–0:10)
Comment your sign now. By the end of this video, you'll know your lucky color, best time, and direction for today.

Scene 2 (0:10–0:20)
Today is a Rooster day. Coming up: one Great Day sign, two Good Days, one sign to take it easy, and all twelve.

Scene 3 (0:20–0:30)
Rooster, Ox and Snake are lucky today, and the Rabbit should slow down. Find your sign in the chapters below.

Scene 4 (0:30–0:40)

Scene 5 (0:40–0:50)
Thursday, 19 November: a Great Day for everyone born in the Year of the Rooster! Let's see what this lucky day brings you.

Scene 6 (0:50–1:00)
Herald of the dawn, the Rooster symbolizes honesty, confidence, and a bright new day. Let it guide you.

Scene 7 (1:00–1:10)
People born in the Year of the Rooster are often described as observant, hard-working, and confident. Small choices bring big luck.

ថ្ងៃនេះជាថ្ងៃឆ្នាំរកា។ បន្តិចទៀត៖ មួយឆ្នាំថ្ងៃល្អខ្លាំង ពីរឆ្នាំថ្ងៃល្អ មួយឆ្នាំគួរសម្រាក និងរាសីទាំងដប់ពីរ។

Scene 3 (0:20–0:30)
ឆ្នាំរកា ឆ្នាំឆ្លូវ និងឆ្នាំម្សាញ់ មានសំណាងថ្ងៃនេះ ហើយឆ្នាំថោះ គួរធ្វើអ្វីៗយឺតៗ។ សូមរករាសីរបស់អ្នក ក្នុងជំពូកខាងក្រោម។

Scene 4 (0:30–0:40)
មុននឹងចាប់ផ្ដើម សូមសរសេររាសីរបស់អ្នក ក្នុងមតិយោបល់ ហើយ subscribe ដើម្បីទទួលជោគជតារៀងរាល់ថ្ងៃ។ សូមចែករំលែកសំណាង។

Scene 5 (0:40–0:50)
ថ្ងៃព្រហស្បតិ៍ ថ្ងៃទីដប់ប្រាំបួន ខែវិច្ឆិកា ជាថ្ងៃល្អខ្លាំង សម្រាប់អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំរកា! តោះ មកមើលថា ថ្ងៃសំណាងនេះ នាំអ្វីមកឲ្យអ្នក។

Scene 6 (0:50–1:00)
មាន់ គឺជាអ្នកប្រកាសពេលព្រឹកព្រលឹម។ ឆ្នាំរកាតំណាងឲ្យភាពស្មោះត្រង់ ទំនុកចិត្ត និងថ្ងៃថ្មីដ៏ភ្លឺស្វាង។ សូមឲ្យវាដឹកនាំអ្នក។

Scene 7 (1:00–1:10)
អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំរកា ជារឿយៗ ត្រូវបានគេស្គាល់ថា ល្អិតល្អន់ ឧស្សាហ៍ព្យាយាម និងជឿជាក់លើខ្លួនឯង។ ជម្រើសតូចៗ នាំសំណាងធំ។

Scene 8 (1:10–1:20)
ថ្ងៃនេះ គឺជាថ្ងៃរបស់អ្នក។ សូមញញឹម ហើយចែករំលែកសំណាងជាមួយនរណាម្នាក់ថ្ងៃនេះ។

Scene 9 (1:20–1:30)
ស្នេហា និងគ្រួសារថ្ងៃនេះ៖ ជាថ្ងៃល្អសម្រាប់ទាក់ទងគ្នា ញ៉ាំអាហារជាមួយគ្នា និងបង្ហាញក្ដីស្រឡាញ់ដោយបើកចំហ។ ថ្ងៃធាតុភ្លើង!

Scene 10 (1:30–1:40)
ការងារថ្ងៃនេះ៖ អតិថិជន និងមិត្តរួមការងារ ឆ្លើយតបល្អចំពោះអ្នក។ ជាថ្ងៃល្អសម្រាប់បង្ហាញគំនិត និងបិទកិច្ចព្រមព្រៀង។

Scene 11 (1:40–1:50)
លុយកាក់ថ្ងៃនេះ៖ ជាថ្ងៃល្អសម្រាប់ចំណូលនឹងនរ។ សូមសន្សំប្រាក់ចំណូលថ្ងៃនេះមួយចំណែកតូច មុននឹងចាយលើខ្លួនឯង។ ជម្រើសតូចៗ នាំសំណាងធំ។

Scene 12 (1:50–2:00)
ពណ៌សំណាងថ្ងៃនេះ គឺពណ៌ត្នោត។ កន្សែង កាបូប ឬខ្សែដៃពណ៌ត្នោត នាំសំណាងមកជិតអ្នក។ វានាំភាពស្ងប់ និងទំនុកចិត្ត មកថ្ងៃរបស់អ្នក។

Scene 13 (2:00–2:10)
ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសឦសាន។ ពេលល្អបំផុត៖ ម៉ោងមួយ ដល់ម៉ោងបី ទៀបភ្លឺ។ សូមធ្វើការងារសំខាន់បំផុតរបស់អ្នក ក្នុងពេលនោះ។

Scene 14 (2:10–2:20)
លេខសំណាង៖ ប្រាំពីរ ប៉ែតសិបប្រាំបី និងប្រាំរយប៉ែតសិបពីរ។ សូមសរសេររាសីរបស់អ្នក ក្នុងមតិយោបល់ ហើយរក្សាទុកវីដេអូនេះ!

Scene 15 (2:20–2:30)
ថ្ងៃសំណាងរបស់ឆ្នាំរកា គឺថ្ងៃព្រហស្បតិ៍ និងថ្ងៃសៅរ៍។ សូមទុកសម្រាប់ផែនការសំខាន់ៗ។ ជម្រើសតូចៗ នាំសំណាងធំ។

Scene 16 (2:30–2:40)
រំលឹកបន្តិច៖ អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំរកា ជួនកាលថ្លែងយោបល់រិះគន់ខ្លាំង ឬត្រង់ពេក។ សូមរក្សាតុល្យភាព។ សូមចែករំលែកសំណាង។

Scene 17 (2:40–2:50)
បន្ទាប់ ឆ្នាំឆ្លូវ៖ ថ្ងៃល្អ! ឆ្នាំឆ្លូវ ស្ថិតក្នុងត្រីកោណសំណាង ជាមួយឆ្នាំរកា។ ថ្ងៃធាតុភ្លើង!

Scene 18 (2:50–3:00)
ថ្ងៃនេះ ល្អសម្រាប់ជួបមនុស្ស និងចាប់ផ្ដើមរឿងល្អៗ។ ស្នេហា៖ សូមចំណាយពេលបន្តិចជាមួយមនុស្សជិតស្និទ្ធ។ សូមរក្សាចិត្តស្ងប់។

Scene 19 (3:00–3:10)
ពណ៌សំណាងគឺពណ៌បៃតង ហើយពេលល្អបំផុតគឺម៉ោងប្រាំបួន ដល់ម៉ោងដប់មួយ ព្រឹក។ ជម្រើសតូចៗ នាំសំណាងធំ។

Scene 20 (3:10–3:20)
ទិសសំណាងគឺទិសអាគ្នេយ៍។ លេខសំណាង៖ បួន ដប់ពីរ និងបួនរយដប់បួន។ ថ្ងៃនេះជាថ្ងៃធាតុភ្លើង ជាថ្ងៃនៃភាពក្លៀវក្លា។

Scene 21 (3:20–3:30)
អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំឆ្លូវ ថ្ងៃនេះ សូមនៅជិតអ្នកកើតឆ្នាំរកា។ សំណាងកាន់តែច្រើន ពេលចែករំលែក។ ថ្ងៃធាតុភ្លើង!

Scene 22 (3:30–3:40)
បន្ទាប់ ឆ្នាំម្សាញ់៖ ថ្ងៃល្អ! ឆ្នាំម្សាញ់ ស្ថិតក្នុងត្រីកោណសំណាង ជាមួយឆ្នាំរកា។ សូមរក្សាចិត្តស្ងប់។

Scene 23 (3:40–3:50)
ថ្ងៃនេះ ល្អសម្រាប់ជួបមនុស្ស និងចាប់ផ្ដើមរឿងល្អៗ។ ស្នេហា៖ សូមចំណាយពេលបន្តិចជាមួយមនុស្សជិតស្និទ្ធ។ សូមចែករំលែកសំណាង។

Scene 24 (3:50–4:00)
ពណ៌សំណាងគឺពណ៌ខ្មៅ ហើយពេលល្អបំផុតគឺម៉ោងមួយ ដល់ម៉ោងបី ទៀបភ្លឺ។ សូមចែករំលែកសំណាង។

Scene 25 (4:00–4:10)
ទិសសំណាងគឺទិសឦសាន។ លេខសំណាង៖ ពីរ កៅសិបពីរ និងប្រាំបួនរយប៉ែតសិបពីរ។ ថ្ងៃនេះជាថ្ងៃធាតុភ្លើង ជាថ្ងៃនៃភាពក្លៀវក្លា។

Scene 26 (4:10–4:20)
អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំម្សាញ់ ថ្ងៃនេះ សូមនៅជិតអ្នកកើតឆ្នាំរកា។ សំណាងកាន់តែច្រើន ពេលចែករំលែក។ សូមរក្សាចិត្តស្ងប់។

Scene 27 (4:20–4:30)
ថ្ងៃនេះ ថ្ងៃទីដប់ប្រាំបួន ខែវិច្ឆិកា ជាថ្ងៃឆ្នាំរកា ទល់មុខឆ្នាំថោះ ក្នុងរង្វង់រាសី។ មានថ្ងៃខ្លះ អ្វីៗមិនដូចការគ្រោងទុកទេ។

Scene 28 (4:30–4:40)
ហើយវាមិនអីទេ។ មនុស្សគ្រប់គ្នា ធ្លាប់មានថ្ងៃបែបនេះ។ ថ្ងៃស្ងប់ស្ងាត់ មិនមែនជាថ្ងៃអាក្រក់ទេ គឺជាថ្ងៃសម្រាប់ដកដង្ហើមវែងៗ។

Scene 29 (4:40–4:50)
សូមប្រយ័ត្នថ្ងៃនេះ៖ ជួនកាលប្រុងប្រយ័ត្នខ្លាំងពេក និងចៀសវាងការប្រឈមមុខដែលចាំបាច់។ សូមឈប់បន្តិច មុននឹងឆ្លើយតប។ សូមមេត្តាខ្លួនឯង។

Scene 30 (4:50–5:00)
សូមទន់ភ្លន់ចំពោះមនុស្សដែលអ្នកស្រឡាញ់ និងចំពោះខ្លួនឯង។ ហើយពឹងលើមិត្តដែលអ្នកទុកចិត្ត។

Scene 31 (5:00–5:10)
ក្នុងការងារ និងលុយកាក់ សូមបោះជំហានតូចៗ ដោយប្រុងប្រយ័ត្ន។ បញ្ចប់រឿងមួយ រួចសឹមធ្វើរឿងបន្ទាប់។ ជំហានតូចៗ ក៏មានតម្លៃដែរ។

Scene 32 (5:10–5:20)
យឺត ក៏នៅតែជាការឈានទៅមុខដែរ។ រាល់ថ្ងៃស្ងប់ស្ងាត់ ធ្វើឲ្យអ្នកកាន់តែរឹងមាំ ហើយថ្ងៃភ្លឺស្វាង កំពុងមកដល់។

Scene 33 (5:20–5:30)
ឆ្នាំជូត៖ ថ្ងៃធម្មតា។ ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌មាស។ ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោងប្រាំពីរ ដល់ម៉ោងប្រាំបួន ព្រឹក។ ថ្ងៃធាតុភ្លើង!

Scene 34 (5:30–5:40)
ស្នេហា៖ ជាថ្ងៃស្ងប់ស្ងាត់សម្រាប់ទំនាក់ទំនង។ លុយកាក់៖ ការចំណាយប្រចាំថ្ងៃ គួររក្សាឲ្យសាមញ្ញ។ ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសអាគ្នេយ៍។ ថ្ងៃធាតុភ្លើង!

Scene 35 (5:40–5:50)
ឆ្នាំខាល៖ ថ្ងៃធម្មតា។ ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌ខៀវ។ ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោងដប់មួយ ព្រឹក ដល់ម៉ោងមួយ រសៀល។

Scene 36 (5:50–6:00)
ស្នេហា៖ ជាថ្ងៃស្ងប់ស្ងាត់សម្រាប់ទំនាក់ទំនង។ ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសខាងត្បូង។ ជម្រើសល្អៗតូចៗ នាំមកនូវថ្ងៃដ៏សំណាង។

Scene 37 (6:00–6:10)
ឆ្នាំរោង៖ ថ្ងៃធម្មតា។ ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌ប្រផេះ។ ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោងដប់មួយ យប់ ដល់ម៉ោងមួយ ទៀបភ្លឺ។

ថ្ងៃធាតុភ្លើង!

62
00:02:50,000 --> 00:02:52,258
ថ្ងៃនេះ ល្អសម្រាប់ជួបមនុស្ស

63
00:02:52,308 --> 00:02:53,967
និងចាប់ផ្ដើមរឿងល្អៗ។

64
00:02:54,017 --> 00:02:54,993
ស្នេហា៖

65
00:02:55,043 --> 00:02:58,326
សូមចំណាយពេលបន្តិចជាមួយមនុស្សជិតស្និទ្ធ។

66
00:02:58,376 --> 00:02:59,950
សូមរក្សាចិត្តស្ងប់។

67
00:03:00,000 --> 00:03:02,102
ពណ៌សំណាងគឺពណ៌បៃតង

68
00:03:02,152 --> 00:03:07,165
ហើយពេលល្អបំផុតគឺម៉ោង 9 ដល់ម៉ោង 11 ព្រឹក។

69
00:03:07,215 --> 00:03:09,950
ជម្រើសតូចៗ នាំសំណាងធំ។

70
00:03:10,000 --> 00:03:12,422
ទិសសំណាងគឺទិសអាគ្នេយ៍។

71
00:03:12,472 --> 00:03:15,006
លេខសំណាង៖ 4 12 និង 414។

72
00:03:15,056 --> 00:03:19,950
ថ្ងៃនេះជាថ្ងៃធាតុភ្លើង ជាថ្ងៃនៃភាពក្លៀវក្លា។

73
00:03:20,000 --> 00:03:22,667
អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំឆ្លូវ ថ្ងៃនេះ

74
00:03:22,717 --> 00:03:25,276
សូមនៅជិតអ្នកកើតឆ្នាំរកា។

75
00:03:25,326 --> 00:03:28,428
សំណាងកាន់តែច្រើន ពេលចែករំលែក។

76
00:03:28,478 --> 00:03:29,950
ថ្ងៃធាតុភ្លើង!

77
00:03:30,000 --> 00:03:32,971
បន្ទាប់ ឆ្នាំម្សាញ់៖ ថ្ងៃល្អ!

78
00:03:33,021 --> 00:03:36,513
ឆ្នាំម្សាញ់ ស្ថិតក្នុងត្រីកោណសំណាង

79
00:03:36,563 --> 00:03:37,971
ជាមួយឆ្នាំរកា។

80
00:03:38,021 --> 00:03:39,950
សូមរក្សាចិត្តស្ងប់។

81
00:03:40,000 --> 00:03:42,298
ថ្ងៃនេះ ល្អសម្រាប់ជួបមនុស្ស

82
00:03:42,348 --> 00:03:44,037
និងចាប់ផ្ដើមរឿងល្អៗ។

83
00:03:44,087 --> 00:03:45,080
ស្នេហា៖

84
00:03:45,130 --> 00:03:48,472
សូមចំណាយពេលបន្តិចជាមួយមនុស្សជិតស្និទ្ធ។

85
00:03:48,522 --> 00:03:49,950
សូមចែករំលែកសំណាង។

86
00:03:50,000 --> 00:03:52,217
ពណ៌សំណាងគឺពណ៌ខ្មៅ

87
00:03:52,267 --> 00:03:57,683
ហើយពេលល្អបំផុតគឺម៉ោង 1 ដល់ម៉ោង 3 ទៀបភ្លឺ។

88
00:03:57,733 --> 00:03:59,950
សូមចែករំលែកសំណាង។

89
00:04:00,000 --> 00:04:02,068
ទិសសំណាងគឺទិសឦសាន។

90
00:04:02,118 --> 00:04:04,774
លេខសំណាង៖ 2 92 និង 982។

91
00:04:04,824 --> 00:04:09,950
ថ្ងៃនេះជាថ្ងៃធាតុភ្លើង ជាថ្ងៃនៃភាពក្លៀវក្លា។

92
00:04:10,000 --> 00:04:12,603
អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំម្សាញ់ ថ្ងៃនេះ

93
00:04:12,653 --> 00:04:15,052
សូមនៅជិតអ្នកកើតឆ្នាំរកា។

94
00:04:15,102 --> 00:04:18,011
សំណាងកាន់តែច្រើន ពេលចែករំលែក។

95
00:04:18,061 --> 00:04:19,950
សូមរក្សាចិត្តស្ងប់។

96
00:04:20,000 --> 00:04:23,824
ថ្ងៃនេះ ថ្ងៃទី 19 ខែវិច្ឆិកា ជាថ្ងៃឆ្នាំរកា

97
00:04:23,874 --> 00:04:26,617
ទល់មុខឆ្នាំថោះ ក្នុងរង្វង់រាសី។

98
00:04:26,667 --> 00:04:29,950
មានថ្ងៃខ្លះ អ្វីៗមិនដូចការគ្រោងទុកទេ។

99
00:04:30,000 --> 00:04:31,090
ហើយវាមិនអីទេ។

100
00:04:31,140 --> 00:04:34,248
មនុស្សគ្រប់គ្នា ធ្លាប់មានថ្ងៃបែបនេះ។

101
00:04:34,298 --> 00:04:37,494
ថ្ងៃស្ងប់ស្ងាត់ មិនមែនជាថ្ងៃអាក្រក់ទេ

102
00:04:37,544 --> 00:04:39,950
គឺជាថ្ងៃសម្រាប់ដកដង្ហើមវែងៗ។

103
00:04:40,000 --> 00:04:41,507
សូមប្រយ័ត្នថ្ងៃនេះ៖

104
00:04:41,557 --> 00:04:43,802
ជួនកាលប្រុងប្រយ័ត្នខ្លាំងពេក

105
00:04:43,852 --> 00:04:46,343
និងចៀសវាងការប្រឈមមុខដែលចាំបាច់។

106
00:04:46,393 --> 00:04:48,557
សូមឈប់បន្តិច មុននឹងឆ្លើយតប។

107
00:04:48,607 --> 00:04:49,950
សូមមេត្តាខ្លួនឯង។

108
00:04:50,000 --> 00:04:54,394
សូមទន់ភ្លន់ចំពោះមនុស្សដែលអ្នកស្រឡាញ់

109
00:04:54,444 --> 00:04:56,370
និងចំពោះខ្លួនឯង។

110
00:04:56,420 --> 00:04:59,950
ហើយពឹងលើមិត្តដែលអ្នកទុកចិត្ត។

111
00:05:00,000 --> 00:05:01,897
ក្នុងការងារ និងលុយកាក់

112
00:05:01,947 --> 00:05:04,817
សូមបោះជំហានតូចៗ ដោយប្រុងប្រយ័ត្ន។

113
00:05:04,867 --> 00:05:07,826
បញ្ចប់រឿងមួយ រួចសឹមធ្វើរឿងបន្ទាប់។

114
00:05:07,876 --> 00:05:09,950
ជំហានតូចៗ ក៏មានតម្លៃដែរ។

115
00:05:10,000 --> 00:05:12,705
យឺត ក៏នៅតែជាការឈានទៅមុខដែរ។

116
00:05:12,755 --> 00:05:17,093
រាល់ថ្ងៃស្ងប់ស្ងាត់ ធ្វើឲ្យអ្នកកាន់តែរឹងមាំ

117
00:05:17,143 --> 00:05:19,950
ហើយថ្ងៃភ្លឺស្វាង កំពុងមកដល់។

118
00:05:20,000 --> 00:05:22,480
ឆ្នាំជូត៖ ថ្ងៃធម្មតា។

119
00:05:22,530 --> 00:05:24,528
ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌មាស។

120
00:05:24,578 --> 00:05:28,263
ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោង 7 ដល់ម៉ោង 9 ព្រឹក។

121
00:05:28,313 --> 00:05:29,950
ថ្ងៃធាតុភ្លើង!

122
00:05:30,000 --> 00:05:33,390
ស្នេហា៖ ជាថ្ងៃស្ងប់ស្ងាត់សម្រាប់ទំនាក់ទំនង។

123
00:05:33,440 --> 00:05:37,070
លុយកាក់៖ ការចំណាយប្រចាំថ្ងៃ គួររក្សាឲ្យសាមញ្ញ។

124
00:05:37,120 --> 00:05:38,830
ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសអាគ្នេយ៍។

125
00:05:38,880 --> 00:05:39,950
ថ្ងៃធាតុភ្លើង!

126
00:05:40,000 --> 00:05:42,750
ឆ្នាំខាល៖ ថ្ងៃធម្មតា។

127
00:05:42,800 --> 00:05:45,017
ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌ខៀវ។

128
00:05:45,067 --> 00:05:49,950
ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោង 11 ព្រឹក ដល់ម៉ោង 1 រសៀល។

129
00:05:50,000 --> 00:05:54,250
ស្នេហា៖ ជាថ្ងៃស្ងប់ស្ងាត់សម្រាប់ទំនាក់ទំនង។

130
00:05:54,300 --> 00:05:56,450
ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសខាងត្បូង។

131
00:05:56,500 --> 00:05:59,950
ជម្រើសល្អៗតូចៗ នាំមកនូវថ្ងៃដ៏សំណាង។

132
00:06:00,000 --> 00:06:02,608
ឆ្នាំរោង៖ ថ្ងៃធម្មតា។

133
00:06:02,658 --> 00:06:05,140
ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌ប្រផេះ។

134
00:06:05,190 --> 00:06:09,950
ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោង 11 យប់ ដល់ម៉ោង 1 ទៀបភ្លឺ។

135
00:06:10,000 --> 00:06:13,363
ស្នេហា៖ ជាថ្ងៃស្ងប់ស្ងាត់សម្រាប់ទំនាក់ទំនង។

136
00:06:13,413 --> 00:06:17,013
លុយកាក់៖ ការចំណាយប្រចាំថ្ងៃ គួររក្សាឲ្យសាមញ្ញ។

137
00:06:17,063 --> 00:06:18,601
ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសខាងជើង។

138
00:06:18,651 --> 00:06:19,950
សូមចែករំលែកសំណាង។

139
00:06:20,000 --> 00:06:22,310
ឆ្នាំមមី៖ ថ្ងៃធម្មតា។

140
00:06:22,360 --> 00:06:24,332
ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌បៃតង។

141
00:06:24,382 --> 00:06:28,040
ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោង 3 ដល់ម៉ោង 5 ទៀបភ្លឺ។

142
00:06:28,090 --> 00:06:29,950
សូមចែករំលែកសំណាង។

143
00:06:30,000 --> 00:06:34,045
ស្នេហា៖ ជាថ្ងៃស្ងប់ស្ងាត់សម្រាប់ទំនាក់ទំនង។

144
00:06:34,095 --> 00:06:35,760
ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសឦសាន។

145
00:06:35,810 --> 00:06:39,950
ថ្ងៃនេះជាថ្ងៃធាតុភ្លើង ជាថ្ងៃនៃភាពក្លៀវក្លា។

146
00:06:40,000 --> 00:06:42,421
ឆ្នាំមមែ៖ ថ្ងៃធម្មតា។

147
00:06:42,471 --> 00:06:44,656
ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌ស្វាយ។

148
00:06:44,706 --> 00:06:48,303
ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោង 5 ដល់ម៉ោង 7 ព្រឹក។

149
00:06:48,353 --> 00:06:49,950
ថ្ងៃធាតុភ្លើង!

150
00:06:50,000 --> 00:06:50,934
ស្នេហា៖

151

សូមចំណាយពេលសាមញ្ញៗជាមួយមនុស្សជិតស្និទ្ធ

152

ដូចជាដើរលេង ឬញ៉ាំអាហារជាមួយគ្នា។

153
00:06:56,803 --> 00:06:58,393
ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសខាងកើត។

154
00:06:58,443 --> 00:06:59,950
សូមរក្សាចិត្តស្ងប់។

155
00:07:00,000 --> 00:07:02,690
ឆ្នាំវក៖ ថ្ងៃធម្មតា។

156
00:07:02,740 --> 00:07:04,745
ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌ស។

157
00:07:04,795 --> 00:07:09,950
ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោង 11 យប់ ដល់ម៉ោង 1 ទៀបភ្លឺ។

158

ស្នេហា៖ ជាថ្ងៃស្ងប់ស្ងាត់សម្រាប់ទំនាក់ទំនង។

159
00:07:14,778 --> 00:07:16,950
ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសខាងជើង។

160
00:07:17,000 --> 00:07:19,950
សូមមើលបន្ត មានដំណឹងល្អៗទៀត។

161
00:07:20,000 --> 00:07:22,109
ឆ្នាំច៖ ថ្ងៃធម្មតា។

162
00:07:22,159 --> 00:07:24,268
ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌ស្វាយ។

163
00:07:24,318 --> 00:07:28,018
ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោង 3 ដល់ម៉ោង 5 ទៀបភ្លឺ។

164
00:07:28,068 --> 00:07:29,950
សូមចែករំលែកសំណាង។

165
00:07:30,000 --> 00:07:33,363
ស្នេហា៖ ជាថ្ងៃស្ងប់ស្ងាត់សម្រាប់ទំនាក់ទំនង។

166
00:07:33,413 --> 00:07:37,013
លុយកាក់៖ ការចំណាយប្រចាំថ្ងៃ គួររក្សាឲ្យសាមញ្ញ។

167
00:07:37,063 --> 00:07:38,442
ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសឦសាន។

168
00:07:38,492 --> 00:07:39,950
សូមរក្សាចិត្តស្ងប់។

169
00:07:40,000 --> 00:07:42,392

170
00:07:42,442 --> 00:07:44,369
ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌លឿង។

171
00:07:44,419 --> 00:07:47,973
ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោង 5 ដល់ម៉ោង 7 ព្រឹក។

172
00:07:48,023 --> 00:07:49,950
សូមចែករំលែកសំណាង។

173

## Keep the voice the same in every scene
Flow makes the voice fresh for every clip, so the voice text in every scene is written to describe exactly the same voice: the same words, in the same order, at the start of the audio line, for every scene and every video. To keep it that way:
- Always use "Copy + English voice" or "Copy + Khmer voice". Do not retype, shorten or translate the voice text, and do not add other words about the voice.
- Make all clips of one video in the same Flow project, with the same model and settings for every clip.
- Use the same language for the whole video (all English or all Khmer).
- Listen to each clip as it is made. If one sounds different, make only that clip again with the same copied prompt.

## Khmer voice – how to get clear Khmer
Every Khmer voice line in this pack is written for speaking: short sentences, a ។ at each pause, and numbers written out in Khmer words (for example ហុកសិបបួន, not 64) so the voice does not read them in English. Subtitles and captions keep the digits.

### If you use Google Flow
Use "Copy + Khmer voice" for each scene; the voice description is already included. Flow's Khmer can be unclear on some clips. Listen to every clip, and remake any clip where words are wrong, cut off or English-sounding. If Khmer stays unclear, make the clips without voice ("Copy") and add a recorded Khmer voice-over instead.

### If you use a text-to-speech app
- Language: Cambodian Khmer (km-KH). Pick a real Khmer voice, for example Microsoft Azure's female voice "km-KH-SreymomNeural" (used by many TTS apps and sites). Do not use an English voice with Khmer text.
- Speed: 0.85× to 0.9×. Pitch: natural. Pause between sentences: normal.
- Paste the Khmer voice-over text from this pack exactly as it is (Khmer script, no romanization).
- Make one scene at a time if the app cuts off long text, then join the clips in your editor.
- Before using a new voice, test it with these two sentences and listen carefully:
  សួស្តីអ្នកទាំងអស់គ្នា! សូមស្វាគមន៍មកកាន់ MyBirthSign.com។
  ថ្ងៃនេះជាថ្ងៃល្អសម្រាប់អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំខ្លា។ សូមជឿជាក់លើខ្លួនឯង ហើយកុំភ្លេចអត់ធ្មត់។
  Check that every word is clear Cambodian Khmer, sentence endings are complete, and speed and volume stay even.

### Record it yourself
A native speaker reading the Khmer voice-over slowly and clearly is always the most natural choice.

## Style (paste first in every scene)
Vertical 9:16. Photorealistic live-action fantasy film, the look of a high-budget fantasy epic that blends Khmer royal heritage with the grandeur of a Chinese historical fantasy drama. Real human faces with natural skin texture, the zodiac characters saved in Flow, each named after its animal (@Rat, @Ox, @Tiger, @Rabbit, @Dragon, @Snake, @Horse, @Goat, @Monkey, @Rooster, @Dog, @Pig): a guardian warrior in ornate armor themed on the animal, with a lifelike animal beside them (realistic anatomy, real fur, feathers or scales), always exactly as in the saved character picture, noble and powerful (never cute toys or cartoons). Setting: a majestic Khmer temple inspired by Angkor Wat with towering carved stone, an enormous luminous full moon, deep indigo starry sky, drifting mist, a still reflecting pool with floating pink lotus flowers and ornate golden lanterns. A living, magical sky in every shot: thin silver clouds drift slowly across the moon, stars twinkle, a soft halo pulses around the moon and sky lanterns rise gently in the distance. Action and magic in the style of a Chinese fantasy (xianxia) drama: graceful wire-fu flying leaps, slow-motion martial-arts moves, flowing robes and capes, glowing sword energy and swirling qi light, always without blood, injury or fighting between characters. Golden magic: shimmering gold particles, flowing ribbons of liquid-gold light and floating gold dust that never hide a face. Physically real lighting: cool silver-blue moonlight, warm golden rim light, soft lantern glow, volumetric light rays; colors deep navy, imperial gold, ruby red and lotus pink. Cinematic depth of field, smooth slow camera movement, high-end visual effects, 4K detail. The zodiac guardians and their animals never talk and never move their mouths; only Chanda, the presenter, speaks. No text, no letters, no subtitles, no watermark, no logos, no cartoon or plastic look, no low-quality CGI.

## Scene 1 – Rooster day (Chanda on camera)
@Chanda, a beautiful Cambodian woman in her early twenties with a warm, kind and confident presence: oval face, soft defined cheekbones, warm amber-brown almond eyes, natural brows, rosy cheeks, natural skin with real texture and refined natural makeup, glossy black hair swept into a low side bun on her right side held by an elaborate golden lotus hairpin with fine dangling gold chains, delicate gold flower earrings, a fitted cream-and-gold Khmer silk brocade blouse with a high mandarin collar and long sleeves, a large round gold medallion on a chain engraved with all twelve zodiac animals, an ornate gold belt with a single ruby-red gemstone at the buckle, a deep navy silk sampot to the ankles with a vertical gold-embroidered front panel and a wide band of Khmer gold patterns at the hem, and gold bangles on both wrists, stands in front of the Angkor-style temple at night, facing the camera and speaking to the viewer with natural lip movements, then turns and gestures toward the pond. She speaks with heartfelt enthusiasm: a joyful smile, bright eyes and lively, natural hand gestures, as if she can't wait to tell the viewer something special. Natural, believable acting with real micro-expressions, never exaggerated or theatrical. Chanda is the saved Flow character @Chanda. Then: magical and powerful. @Rooster (the Rooster guardian with their rooster) stands on its glowing lotus pedestal on the pond in front of the Angkor-style temple under a huge full moon. Its eyes light up, a golden aura bursts around it, and it glides down from the sky onto its guardian's arm as the guardian sweeps a glowing sword in a wide arc, leaving a trail of shining feathers, as today's Fire magic swirls around them: warm red-gold embers, small dancing flame-petals and rising sparks that never burn anything. The lotus pond ripples with golden light, and a shower of tiny stars falls over the water. Dynamic camera: start close on its face, then sweep up and follow it through the air. Cute, majestic and full of energy, matching the reference image.

## Scene 2 – Lucky signs
Magical and powerful. @Rooster (the Rooster guardian with their rooster), @Ox (the Ox guardian with their ox) and @Snake (the Snake guardian with their snake) fly in from three directions on trails of golden light and land together on glowing lotus pedestals in front of the temple. As they land, thousands of fireflies burst up from the reeds and circle them like stars. They look at each other and cheer. The camera orbits around them in a smooth circle. The animals match the reference images.

## Scene 3 – Rabbit, take it easy
Photorealistic golden fantasy-epic style, same saved Flow characters (Chanda and the zodiac characters named after their animals). @Rabbit (the Rabbit guardian with their rabbit) rests on a lotus pedestal by the pond: it lies down on a soft bed of petals as the moonlight wraps it in a gentle glow, and the full moon shines calmly on the still water. The mood is peaceful, safe and magical, not sad. The camera moves in slowly. The rabbit is the saved Flow character @Rabbit.

## Scene 4 – Outro
Magical and powerful. A gentle shower of shooting stars falls behind the temple towers, and the huge full moon glows over the pond. The camera rises up and pulls back for a wide, epic view, leaving open sky at the top of the frame for a title.

## Scene 5 – Lucky numbers (about 10–12 seconds)
Magical and powerful. A huge glowing golden zodiac wheel rises out of the lotus pond in front of the temple, with the twelve golden-armored zodiac guardians around its rim. It spins faster and faster, shooting beams of golden light and sparkles, then slows to a gentle, shining turn under the full moon. The camera slowly pushes in, keeping open space in the middle and lower part of the frame for the number captions. (Loop the slow-turning part, or slow-zoom a still picture, to fill the time.)

Flow (Veo) can make the voice and music together with the picture. Add ONE of these lines to the END of each scene prompt: the English one for the English video, the Khmer one for the Khmer video. Each line is short enough for an 8-second clip.
Put the clips in this order in your editor: 1, 2, 3, 5 (lucky numbers), 4 (ending).
Voice style (keep it the same every day so the channel has one recognisable voice): identical in every clip of this series (the same woman, same timbre, same pitch, same accent, same speed, same energy): a young Cambodian woman in her mid-twenties speaking clear, natural English with a neutral accent, medium-high pitch, a soft, warm and bright tone, a gentle smile in her voice, the same calm, cheerful energy from the first word to the last (never louder, faster, deeper, older or more dramatic), a steady pace of about 150 words per minute, even volume, recorded close to a good studio microphone in a quiet room with no echo or reverb; no whispering, no singing, no other voices

00:00:05,914 --> 00:00:07,250
នេះគឺជោគជតារាសី

4
00:00:07,300 --> 00:00:10,853
សម្រាប់ថ្ងៃទី 19 ខែវិច្ឆិកា ឆ្នាំ 2026។

5
00:00:10,903 --> 00:00:12,886
ថ្ងៃនេះជាថ្ងៃឆ្នាំរកា។

6
00:00:12,936 --> 00:00:16,028
ឆ្នាំរកា ឆ្នាំឆ្លូវ និងឆ្នាំម្សាញ់

7
00:00:16,078 --> 00:00:18,430
មានសំណាងល្អជាងគេនៅថ្ងៃនេះ។

8
00:00:18,480 --> 00:00:21,757
ចំណែកឆ្នាំថោះ គួរសម្រាកឲ្យស្រួលខ្លួន

9
00:00:21,807 --> 00:00:22,958
ធ្វើអ្វីៗយឺតៗ

10
00:00:23,008 --> 00:00:26,007
និងកុំប្រញាប់ក្នុងការសម្រេចចិត្ត។

11
00:00:26,057 --> 00:00:30,073
សម្រាប់លេខសំណាងថ្ងៃនេះ៖ ឆ្នាំរកា 88 និង 582។

12
00:00:30,123 --> 00:00:32,106
ឆ្នាំឆ្លូវ 12 និង 414។

13
00:00:32,156 --> 00:00:34,231
ឆ្នាំម្សាញ់ 92 និង 982។

14
00:00:34,281 --> 00:00:36,449
សូមសរសេរលេខសំណាងរបស់អ្នក

15
00:00:36,499 --> 00:00:39,406
នៅក្នុងមតិយោបល់ ហើយនិយាយម្ដងទៀត!

16
00:00:39,456 --> 00:00:43,564
ចូលមើលលេខសំណាង និងលម្អិតសម្រាប់រាសីទាំង 12 នៅ

17
00:00:43,614 --> 00:00:44,950
mybirthsign.com
```
